UWF (23 Mar 1996) — Card

UWF Gold Rush 2004

5 June 2004
Content noticeA period document, served as recovered; withheld details are marked. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
[Fade up from black, to the UWF logo with the words "Established 1996" underneath it. After a few moments of silence, the voice of Fergie from the Black Eyed Peas comes in.] # Let's get it started in here... # [And now images of the past eight years come rolling past, each as a black and white image over the pulsating bass beat of "Let's Get It Started" by the Black Eyed Peas...] # And the bass keeps runnin, runnin and runnin runnin' # [...Serge Annis holding the UWF Universal Heavyweight Championship over his head in the first Gold Rush Main Event... Members of Legion brawling with the original Pride back in 1997...] # and runnin' runnin' and runnin' runnin' and runnin' runnin' # [...from 1998 as "Crimson" Joe Reed stands victorious over Annis in the iron man contest... a shot from 1999 with Scott "Hotspot" Daniels standing with half of the tag team championship in his yearly title win... 2000 is next with a shot of Paul Wong powerbombing Gus Zampitella from the height of the Unitron to the floor 20 feet below...] # and runnin' runnin' and runnin' runnin, and runnin' runnnin' # [...And now we see the highlight of 2001 as Chris O'Brien gets the last laugh over the Machines a year later by slapping the Aristoclutch on to take the tag team titles... a year later as 2002's event is capped off by Quinn Brown hitting the Fatal Hesitation en route to winning the world championship... and now to last year with all four main event participants brawling on top of EW4's limousine...] # and... # [...now a quick shot of Edmond Winston IV's summit with the announcement of Marshall Law, followed up by the reformation of the Youngbloods a few weeks ago...] # In this context, there's no disrespect # # So when I bust my rhyme # [...a Gone To Texas by Kyle Lee on Edmond Winston...] # You break ya necks # [...Serge Annis and Caliban hit the NDE on Brian McKenzie...] # We got five minutes for us to disconnect # # From all intellect and let the rhythm effect # [...Alex Extreme and Alex Martinez standing back to back as the Pride encircle them in the aisleway...] # Obstacles and inhibition follow your intuition # # Free your inner soul and break away from tradition # [Angel lays out Allison Ivey back in the locker rooms with a HeadHunter... Myra Benedict hits a crescent kick on Leslie Hernandez...] # Cause when we be out, girlies pull the heat out # [Sonya Benedict hits Chad Grimsson with a low blow...] # You wouldn't believe how we wow shit out # # Burnin 'til it's burned out, turn it 'til it's turned out # # Act up from North West East South # [...Victor Frost hits Quinn Brown with a piledriver... Scott Daniels eats a Firebomb Chokeslam from The Last American Badass...] # Everybody, everybody, let's get into it. # # Get stupid. # # Get it started, get it started, Get it started, # [...Jamie Underwood and November each sail over the ropes onto prone opponents... Randall Osbourne blasts Alex Kidd in the back of the head with a chair...] # Let's get it started, (HA) # # Let's get it started, (In Here) # [...Michael Bonn drives the One-Winged Angel's head into a wall in the men's room...] # Let's get it started, (HA) # # Let's get it started, (In Here) # [...Frost is saved by his valets after being put through the windshield by Quinn...] # Let's get it started, (HA) # # Let's get it started, (In Here) # [...Michael Augustine drives Alex Extreme into the ground with a Soul Breaker... The Pride and New Era come face to face in EW4's private suite...] # Let's get it started, (HA) # # Let's get it started, (In Here) # # Yeah. # [...the Misfits rough up Jessica "Fatality" Marshall in the ring... Pierce Bleed charges into the ring and levels Uriah Hawkins with a lariat...] # Lose control, up outta your soul # # Don't move to fast, people, just take it slow # # Don't get ahead, just jump into it # # Y'all hear about it, the peas will do it # [...Strictly Business rises up out of their wheelchairs to assault Team Canada with a flurry of punches and kicks... 1WA drives Bonn to the mat with a Revelator...] # Get started, get stupid # # Don't worry about it, people will walk you through it # # Step by step like your into new kid # # Inch by inch with the new solution # [...Stephanie Harper dives over the ropes, knocking into Donna Tetreault as well as Simon O'Neal... Tetreault celebrating the improbable title win over Harper moments later and the referee finding the brass knuckles...] # Transmit hits with no delusion # # The feeling's irresistable and thats how we move it # [...the Machines face to face in the locker rooms, their bond broken... the Keenings coming to the rescue of McKenzie and into battle with Annis and Caliban...] # Everybody, everybody, let's get into it. # # Get stupid. # # Get it started, get it started, Get it started, # # Let's get it started, (HA) # # Let's get it started, (In Here) # [...Hotspot and Alex Martinez staring at each other with hatred...] # Let's get it started, (HA) # # Let's get it started, (In Here) # [...Brown and Frost trading blows in the backstage area...] # Let's get it started, (HA) # # Let's get it started, (In Here) # [...Augustine holding the championship belt and Winston standing across from him in the ring, Lee in the middle of the aisle...] # Let's get it started, (HA) # # Let's get it started, (In Here) # # Yeah. # # Runnin' runnin' and runnin' runnin' and runnin' runnin' # [The lyrics begin to echo over and over as we dissolve into the PPV graphics...] _ ___ ________ | | | \ \ / / ____| | | | |\ \ /\ / /| |__ | | | | \ \/ \/ / | __| | |__| | \ /\ / | | \____/ \/ \/ |_| ________ .__ ._____________ .__ / _____/ ____ | | __| _/\______ \ __ __ ______| |__ / \ ___ / _ \ | | / __ | | _/| | \ / ___/| | \ \ \_\ \( <_> )| |__/ /_/ | | | \| | / \___ \ | Y \ \______ / \____/ |____/\____ | |____|_ /|____/ /____ >|___| / \/ ________ ____\__ _____\/ ________ \/ \/ \_____ \ \ _ \ \ _ \ \_____ \ / ____/ / /_\ \ / /_\ \ _(__ < / \ \ \_/ \\ \_/ \ / \ \_______ \ \_____ / \_____ //______ / \/ \/ \/ \/ .-----------------------------------------------------------------. | Live! Saturday June 5th 2004 Pontiac Silverdome, Detroit, MI | | Hour One | `-----------------------------------------------------------------' [A slow dissolve into a camera view, taken high above the roof of the Pontiac Silverdome from a helicopter. The shot twirls around over the fibreglass roof for a few lingering seconds, then another dissolve straight into the stadium itself. A wide-angled shot from the rafters of the building displays the enormous crowd on hand for the pay-per-view, as the audience seems to stretch on for miles. Daylight shines through the roof as the sea of humanity lets loose a tremendous roaring pop! A wrestling right sits in the middle of the floor, with a black ring apron and "Gold Rush 2004" printed on all four sides and a slate grey canvas on the ring mat. Around the ring are black dasher boards that lead to the double wide, double long rampway. At the top of the ramp is a large stage area, a good ten feet off of the arena floor, and the backdrop consists of a series of metal beams and riggings. On each side of the stage are large videoscreens, with the Silverdome's own DiamondVision screen acting as the main screen high above the entrance portal. Standing in the middle of the ring is the UWF's longtime ring announcer, Debbie "Debs" Henshall in a gold satin evening gown.] DH: Ladies and gentlemen, please welcome the broadcast team of the Universal Wrestling Federation... SAM STEELEY! [Over the PA system comes AC/DC's "Big Balls", as a wave of cheers mixed in with booing comes over the giant crowd. Sam emerges from the back in his usual Gold Rush attire, a tuxedo save for his traditional leather jacket. Sam marches down the rampway, pausing briefly to gasp for breath halfway down and then continues to the ringside area.] DH: The 2004 Uni Award winner for Best Announcer... AMY MARSHALL! [Now it's Everclear's "Brown Eyed Girl" that comes over the speakers to a wave of cheers, and Amy Marshall comes out to a chant of "MAR-SHALL! MAR-SHALL!" in a sleek black and red gown, her long hair hanging past her shoulders. Amy walks the rampway, waving to the crowd and reaching out to shake hands with some of the people in the crowd before finally walking to ringside and sitting down next to Sam.] DH: And last but not least... DAVID ROGERS! [And "Ironman" by Ozzy Osbourne starts up to a huge pop as David Rogers, bedecked in a black tuxedo and wire rimmed glasses, enters the Silverdome and heads down the ramp with a roll of papers in his hand. He too waves to the crowd, and then takes his place at ringside.] DR: HELLO EVERYBODY, WELCOME TO GOLD RUSH! [As Debs departs the ring, a loud boom preceeds a blinding pyrotechnics display! Flash after flash of fireworks casts shadows on the sea of people, and the crowd noise reaches dangerous decibel levels. Rockets of pyro stream down to the stage and explode on impact, and we return to the quick cuts all over the building as the voice of the UWF comes in over the scene.] DR: On this, the eighth annual Gold Rush spectacle, we have one of the biggest lineups ever! The biggest stars in the wrestling business are gathered here for the biggest night in our industry! More than one hundred thousand fans packed into the Silverdome, and millions more are watching worldwide! [A pan over the crowd on the floor, the large contingent of Fortress Fraternity partially hidden behind a wall of Pinkerton guards courtesy of one Jessica "Fatality" Marshall. Nevertheless, various message boards and signs capture the attention of an industry for a split second: "Last Chance, Sabby!" -- "Martin, You Should'a Gone To Texas!" -- "Allison, I Love Your Young Blood!" -- "R.I.P. Hotspot 2004" --- "Give Me A Job, EW4!" Cut to the announcers seated at ringside.] DR: Hello again and welcome to Gold Rush 2004! Without question, this night will go down as one of the biggest nights in the wrestling industry, and one of the biggest nights of the year. AM: They all come and go... The Birthday Bashes... The Dead By Dawns... The Showtimes... The Caged Rages... But the grand pinnacle, the cream of the crop, has been and always will be Gold Rush! DR: Indeed, the eighth edition of Gold Rush should prove to be a stellar show. SS: I said it last year and I'll say it again, if you want to work on the biggest stage the business has to offer you have to do it at Gold Rush. DR: With thirteen matches on the slate this year, there should be no shortage of thrills and excitement. And what a way to start things off, a bout that should be nothing short of a knock-down drag-out brawl between the manager of the Youngbloods, Allison Ivey, and former women's tag team champion Angel. AM: On paper this one looks like a total mismatch, Allison Ivey is not a wrestler and has never claimed to be. But as they're quite fond of throwing back in my face... if a Youngblood breathes, they fight. SS: Yeah, but tonight Angel might just try to stop Allison from breathing. DR: Let's head down to the ring for Gold Rush 2004's opening contest, here's Debbie Henshall. ____ ____ _ ___ ____ _ _ ____ _ _ ..............| __ | | | | \ |__/ | | [__ |__| |__] |__| |___ |__/ | \ |__| ___] | |................ ____ ___ ___ _ _ |___ \ / _ \ / _ \ | || | WOMEN'S GRUDGE MATCH: __) || | | || | | || || |_ Allison Ivey versus Angel / __/ | |_| || |_| ||__ _| WRITER: [name withheld] |_____| \___/ \___/ |_| .......................................................................... DH: The first contest of the evening is scheduled for one fall! Introducing first, representing the Youngbloods... ["Limb From Limb" by Motorhead starts up to a mixed response, as the Youngbloods logo is flashed up on the DiamondVision screen. The Children of Evolution shows its appreciation, as everyone else unleashes a heavy round of boos.] DH: From Fort Carswell, Texas... ALLLLLLLLISOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOON IIIIIIIIIIVEYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYY! [Allison enters the building dressed in black kickboxing boots, matching athletic shorts and a white Nike sports bra complete with blood red swoosh. Her hair is pulled up into a ponytail, and the outfit is completed with black fingerless gloves. Ivey comes down the rampway to the ring, ignoring the jeers all around her and slides into the ring as her music fades out.] AM: I don't know what sort of match this is going to be, but any Youngblood involvement pretty much means it'll be one hell of a fight. SS: Don't underestimate the importance of setting the right tone for the night, the Youngbloods want to go three-for-three tonight. [The standard arena lights give way to darkness as a familiar prelude creks over the arena's speakers.] # With every breath, the air grows stale # # Deathly cold winds howl and wail # # Raging thunder pounds like drums # # When something wicked this way comes # [The final word trails off into the abyss... and nothing happens. The crowd sits in unlit confusion for a few seconds before an infernal wall of fire erupts at the entrance with a thunderous boom and Midnight Syndicate's "Witching Hour". The blaze dies down slightly, burning to about knee height, revealing a lone hooded figure standing behind it, wrapped in a floor-length black cloak with its head bowed.] DH: And her opponent! From parts unknown, weighing one hundred and twenty-eight pounds... AAAAAAAAAAAANGEEEEEEEEEEELLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLL! DR: Why is Angel just standing there? SS: Appreciate a good entrance for what it is, Rogers. [After a few more seconds, the wall of flames begins to rise up again. Shortly after Angel walks right through the flames, protected by her cloak as Allison watches from the ring, unimpressed and stretching her hamstring on the top rope. Angel takes her time coming down to the ring, and once she's on the ringsteps removes the cloak to reveal her wrestling attire. She ducks into the ring and Allison immediately walks towards her, but the referee gets in between the two women first and calls for the bell.] DR: And here we go with the first action of Gold Rush 2004! Allison with a kick right to the thigh, backing Angel up to the ropes. Another kick, Angel blocks it- and a legsweep to the mat! AM: Allison's not staying down, pops back up and a shot to Angel's stomach! [The Youngbloods manager directs another kick into the side of Angel's ribcage, and then throws her into the opposite ropes. Angel ducks a roundhouse kick and comes back with a spinning backfist to the stomach, doubling her opponent up. Ivey gasps for breath, and is caught with a mandible claw straight into a monkeyflip takedown where Angel straddles her foe.] DR: The Wail of the Banshee leaves Angel in control of this match, as a series of punches is doing the damage. And now some sort of submission hold, Angel's pulling Ivey up into a sitting position with a neck wrench. AM: She's known for her creative submission holds, and that just looks painful. SS: Well what did you expect, that it'd tickle? [Angel releases the hold and rises to her feet, using a sharp elbow to the back of the neck to bring Ivey up into a double underhook. Allison struggles to escape and does grab the ropes, preventing a backbreaker and uses the referee's count to escape. A thumb to the eye lets Ivey open up with a sharp kick to the gut and then a clothesline knocks her down. Allison tries for a pinfall... 1! 2! Shoulder up!] DR: No! That's the first attempted pin of the night, but Angel manages to escape it rather easily. SS: She's not gonna be put out by a clothesline, you'll have to break her legs or smother her or something. AM: That sounds like foreplay to Angel. [Allison drags Angel up by the hair and tightens her grip with a headlock, but very soon Angel shoves Allison out and surprises her with a release northern lights suplex! As the Texan sits up with some shock, Angel delivers a neck snap to keep her in pain and then applies an STF hold. Once again, Allison struggles to the ropes to break the hold as the crowd boos.] SS: I don't get it, who's the crowd backing here? AM: As long as they both leave the ring pretty sore, this crowd will be happy. Youngblood, Ex-Sabbat Justice, I don't think it matters either way. DR: Angel with a pair of forearm blasts into the spine, scoop up across her shoulders- Death Valley Driver across her knee! Allison's upper chest has got to be in pain! SS: No fair, that's my favorite part! [Angel drags Ivey into the center of the ring and pulls her up slowly, hooking her head under her arm, and lifts Allison straight up into the air for a brainbuster suplex! Angel drives her into the mat with great force, leaving Ivey a twitching mess as Angel applies a seated abdominal stretch hold. The Youngblood's eyes bulge out of her head as she struggles to resist the increasing pressure on her chest and shoulder, and Angel demands that the referee check her opponent.] AI: [Meep] No! DR: Once a Youngblood... [The crowd suddenly unleashes a heel pop, as a figure makes his way out from the back.] AM: Oh no, Edward Drake is on his way down to ringside! [The CoE erupts as one half of the Privateers, Edward, walks towards the ring as Angel releases the STF hold and kneels with all of her weight into the middle of Ivey's back while pulling her arms backwards. Allison groans in pain but still refuses to submit, and Angel soon pulls her up and throws her into the nearest corner sternum-first. With as much force as she can muster, Angel rushes after her and hits a jumping avalanche to further weaken Allison. She slumps against the turnbuckles, but Angel grabs her and whips her cross-corner to the other side of the ring, where she hits another jumping avalanche splash! Outside the ring Edward begins to cheer Allison on, attempting to provide his fellow Youngblood some encouragement.] AM: Not that I'm an Angel booster by any stretch, but Edward doesn't belong out here. SS: He's just out for some moral support, that's all. AM: He's a Youngblood. Morals aren't their style. [Allison struggles to her knees and Angel applies a facelock, dragging her back into the middle of the ring. A quick jab gives Allison just the opening she needs and a tackle brings both of them to the mat. Allison gets in several quick punches as Angel covers up, and Ivey grabs the ropes to lift herself up, gets into position and catches Angel with a swinging kick across the upper left thigh. Angel collapses against the ropes and Allison grabs both of her legs, snapping her neck in between the second and top rope with a guillotine! Seconds later Allison collapses as well, the adrenaline starting to wear off. Nearby, Edward claps his hands and continues shouting words of encouragement to his manager.] DR: For someone without proper wrestling training, Allison Ivey has put up a tremendous fight here tonight against a more experienced and qualified opponent in Angel. AM: If Angel loses this match, I can't even begin to imagine what kind of mood she'll be in from here on out. She attacked several people without provocation before, just think what she'll do now. [Allison gets back up first, and another sweeping kick knocks Angel up against the ropes. Ivey rushes at her with a spear into the ropes, and tries to use the rebounding momentum to suplex her over. Angel shifts her weight however and twists around to plant Allison into the canvas-] If Angel catches a kick, she'll step in and lift Allison as if for a spinebuster but instead drop to her knees and hit something of an inverted Derailer (you remember A-Train using that move, right?) DR: HEADHUNTER! Allison Ivey goes down in a heap! ONE! TWO! THREE! [As the crowd unleashes a wave of boos, Midnight Syndicate's "Witching Hour" hits the PA system again. Angel rises up, holding her ribs with an arm raised in victory. Across the ring, Edward Drake grabs the timekeeper's chair out from underneath him and slides it into the ring, thrusting the chair at Allison's crumpled form.] AM: I knew it was more than moral support for him to be out here dammit! DR: Angel wins the match but she doesn't have any clue what's going on behind her! DH: Here is your winner... AAAAAAANGEEEEEEEEEEEELLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLL! [Drake rounds the corner of the ring and stands in front of Angel to catch her attention, sneering and talking trash at her. Angel stares at Edward, her eyes narrowing- _CRACK!_ But a chairshot from Ivey later, and Angel collapses to the mat. Still dazed, she walks around Angel and winds up again- _CRACK!_ Driving the chair into Angel's face! HEEL POP! With that done, Allison tosses the chair aside and rolls out of the ring. With Edward's assistance she walks back up the aisle, looking glassy-eyed but pissed off. The Youngblood fans roar with delight, as everyone else showers the duo with boos.] DR: We're just one match through Gold Rush and already I'm disgusted as to the actions of the Youngbloods. Angel defeated Allison, and that cowardly post-match attack was not necessary! SS: Remember I said the Youngbloods wanted to start the night off right? She might not've won, but Allison definitely started Gold Rush off properly for her team. [Angel begins to come to, and the official starts to help her up but gets shoved aside. The winner of the match exits the ring on her own, and limps up the rampway under her own power to a mixed cheer. Angel dissapears through the portal, and we cut back to the announcers.] AM: Please don't tell me every match is going to wind up like that last one tonight, all because of this damned Marshall Law. SS: If you don't like it, go to a WNBA game. DR: Well, this next match is going to be intensely personal for sure. Jamie Underwood taking on the man who's life he ruined, Sabbath! AM: Yeah... how can it be anything BUT a fight? Jamie had Sabby's KIDS taken from him for God's sakes! SS: Hey, they're better off without the big dumb bastard! DR: It's going to be one of those nights. Fans, this is Sabbath's FIRST match back since August of 2003, and his first UWF match since February 2002! Both of these guys have been quiet all evening, so let's go down to Debbie and get this one going! [Fade to the ring, where Debs waits.] ____ ____ _ ___ ____ _ _ ____ _ _ ..............| __ | | | | \ |__/ | | [__ |__| |__] |__| |___ |__/ | \ |__| ___] | |................ ____ ___ ___ _ _ |___ \ / _ \ / _ \ | || | SINGLES MATCH: __) || | | || | | || || |_ Sabbath versus Jamie Underwood / __/ | |_| || |_| ||__ _| WRITER: [name withheld] |_____| \___/ \___/ |_| .......................................................................... DH: Ladies and gentlemen, the following contest is scheduled for ONE fall! Introducing first, weighing in at two hundred and fifteen pounds... ["Nightrain" by Guns 'N' Roses kicks on over the P.A. to a massive round of boos as a lone spotlight shines on the stage. A few moments into the song, three figures step out from the back, and the boos seem to increase in intensity, if that's even possible.] DH: ...accompanied to the ring by Sonya Benedict and Alex Price... here is "THE SAVIOR" JAMIE UNDEEEEEEEEEEERWOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOD! [Jamie spreads his arms wide as his name is called, turning to face the fans in all directions, before beginning the long walk to the ring, jawing with the fans on the way, and stopping to pose with a small, albeit devoted, group of Underwood fans at ringside.] AM: Oh God, I can't believe people are actually buying into this guy. DR: Hey, it's about four people... and they're probably homeless and being paid in liquor. AM: Point. Not to mention those Pinkerton guards are okay with them but not Fortress Fraternity. SS: It's called selective betterment. [After satisfying the few in attendance not screaming for his head, Jamie slips into the ring, where he cockily takes the microphone from Debs, blowing a kiss at her, then turns to face the cameras, a grin a mile wide on his face as "Nightrain" fades out.] JU: Yes, yes, you're welcome! Detroit, your Savior has arrived! ["YOU SUCK! YOU SUCK! YOU SUCK!"] JU: Now I'm gonna be brief here... [Big pop, which seems to throw Jamie off slightly.] JU: ...so we can get on to the asskicking you are about to have bestowed upon you by your Savior... but I have an announcement to make. By the order of Jessica Marshall, this match is now NO DISQUALIFICATIONS! Thank you! [MEGA POP! as Jamie tosses the mic back to Debbie.] AM: Is this kid CRAZY?! Making this a no disqualification match against Sabby?! Sabby's already gonna kill him as it is for bringing his son out here! SS: Bah... Jamie's got a plan, as always. He's got Sabby's number tonight! DH: ANNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNND HIS OPPONENT! [The slow, haunting tune to "Turn the Page," as done by Metallica, drifts over the P.A. as the lights darken, the crowd coming to their feet! On the Univision, a heavily stylized anime style cartoon is shown, with a man in a long black trenchcoat and raven black hair standing on the edge of a skyscraper.] # On a long and lonesome highway, easy of Omaha # # You can listen to the engine moanin' out its one note song # # And ya think about the woman, the girl ya knew the night before # [And with that line, the song kicks in full swing, the cartoon replaced by live action footage of the man in black drilling "Candyman" Damien Rose to the mat with a Rocker Dropper, followed by the words "WALKING CONTRADICITON". From beneath the Univision step two figures, as on the big screen the man in black is shown diving off the big screen onto Edward Love. The taller male is wearing full length black tights with red and white tribal designs down each leg, and matching boots, as well as a sleeveless black t-shirt which reads "BEAUTY IS EPHEMERAL, PAIN IS ETERNAL." His companion, the uber-pixie herself, is wearing her typical knee high boots, black skirt, and blouse, and is carrying her favorite toy: a Singapore cane.] # But your thoughts will soon be wanderin', the way they always do # # When you're riding sixteen hours, and there's nothin' much to do # # And ya don't feel much like ridin', ya just wish the trip was through # DH: ...from Seattle, Washington, weighing in at two hundred and sixty-seven pounds, accompanied by Sayaka Daiken-Price... "THE WALKING CONTRADICTION" SSSSSSSSSSABBAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAATH! [The crowd pops massively as Sayaka passes the cane to her husband, who lifts it in Jamie's direction, his eyes cold and steely. As the Sabby on the Univision drills Underwood with a Coffin Breaker II, he makes his way down the aisle, barely acknowledging the outstretched hands.] # Here I am, on the road again # # There I am, up on the stage # # There I go, playin' the star again # # There I go, turn the page # [Sabbath reaches the ringside area, pausing only to remove his shirt, which he tosses to the crowd. He kisses his wife and grins at his son, who smiles back, showing off his "Sabbath" shirt. Sabbath then slowly climbs into the ring, keeping his eyes on Jamie the entire time as the music fades.] DR: This one's not going to be pretty, folks. There's a lot of bad blood between these two. SS: Sabbath started it. AM: HOW?! SS: He's ugly to the point of being offensive to a fine young specimen like Jamie! AM: Ooookay. [Before she can leave the ring, Jamie calls Debbie over, whispering in her ear. She argues with him momentarily, then sighs a resigned sigh before returning to the center of the ring.] DH: Ladies and gentlemen, may I have your attention please? Mr. Underwood has informed me that Sayaka has been BARRED FROM RINGSIDE at the order of Jessica Marshall! AM: THE HELL!?! What about Sonya Benedict?! SS: What about her, Red? She's a perfect angel? [Oh man, does the crowd let Jamie have it as Sabbath about comes unglued, trying to shove Jim Jonas aside to get at the cocky youngster! Sonya shushes Sayaka away, outside the ring, as several referees come down to escort the young lady away.] DR: It looks like Jamie's trying to stack the deck against Sabbath here! Sonya's not going to back down from Sabbath, and won't hesitate to get involved I'm sure! AM: This really isn't fair, but I think all this is doing is fueling Sabby's fire! [Finally, Junas manages to get Sabbath into his corner, signaling for the bell.] DR: And we're underway! AM: Sabbath wasting no time going after Jamie here as...what the heck is Jamie doing?! [Before Sabbath can get to him, Jamie ducks outside the ring, over near Sonya as Junas pushes Sabbath away, struggling with the massive man all while trying to get Jamie into the ring.] SS: So he's not ready! What's the big deal? He's got all night to beat on Sabby! DR: I think all he's doing is just trying to rile the big man up, and I'm sure it's working! SS: Of course it is! Jamie's a genius! [Sabbath reluctantly backs away, grinding his teeth as Jamie cautiously climbs onto the apron, not taking his eyes off his opponent, and as Sabby goes after him again, Jamie hops back down to safer territory as Junas pushes Sabbath back again!] AM: C'mon you little wimp! Get in the ring and be a man! DR: I'm inclined to agree with you. For all his bravado, Jamie's deathly afraid of Sabbath! SS: Well, do you think he WANTS to be here?! All he's concerned about is the welfare of those poor kids! The fact that Sabbath wanted this match just proves that Jamie was right about him! He's a terrible father! AM: Sam, so help me God... that's not funny. If you had the sperm count to produce kids, you'd understand why Sabbath feels the way he does! SS: If I had kids, I'd move to another country! Little bastards always wanting food here, clothes here! Sabbath should be grateful! [SLAP!] DR: [sigh] And Jamie STILL refuses to get in the ring! [Finally, Sabbath tosses Junas aside, tearing through the ropes as Jamie takes off around the ring, Sabbath hot on his trail! Jamie slides into the ring, followed closely by Sabbath! Jamie hits the ropes and tries to come off with a clothesline, but Sabbath ducks and comes back with a spinning leg lariat, taking Jamie down to a massive pop! Jamie is up quickly, however, but is met with several hard shots from Sabbath, sending him into the corner!] DR: Sabbath just laying into Jamie here! This young punk has pushed his luck a little too far tonight! AM: I personally am going to enjoy this! [As Jamie reels from the early onslaught, Sabbath digs his fingers into Jamie's hair, grabbing two big handfuls, then flings Jamie out of the corner by his locks, throwing him halfway across the ring and sliding into the other corner... ....crotch first! Saturday June 5th 2004 Pontiac Silverdome, Detroit, MI | | Hour Three | `-----------------------------------------------------------------' [Fade back up into the interior of the Pontiac Silverdome, as the massive building is now facing the end of natural light for the day with the rapidly darkening sky pouring in through the fibreglass roof. Compensating are the several dozen floodlights which provide more than enough light for this event, as we pan over the capacity crowd once again. Down at ringside, we find that Kidd and Powers have left the ringside area, and Randall Osbourne is in the midst of carrying a still unconscious Cameo back up the ramp to jeers from the nearby fans. And Fortress Fraternity, led by the Grimsson brothers, chimes in too... "DROP HER ASS! DROP HER ASS! DROP HER ASS! DROP HER ASS!" Dissolve back to the announce trio, as all wear smiles except for Sam.] DR: Welcome back, folks, to hour three of this spectacular pay-per-view event! We're halfway through Gold Rush, and what show it's been up to this point. Some real wars, some great wrestling competition, and the best is still yet to come. SS: [his mood brightening] We've got Serge, we've got the Pride, and we've got the guy up next, the Nighthawk! DR: Correct, the next match on the schedule is the cruiserweight championship title match, between Michael Bonn and the One-Winged Angel. SS: Why it's even- ["Bleed for Me" by the Black Label Society interrupts the next slapable offense by Sam. Well, not so much the song. More like the crowd noise. And it doesn't actually stop Sam from saying anything. You just can't hear it. Of course, Amy has a sixth sense about this sort of things... SLAP! And so, Sam's face red with Amy's handprint, out comes the likely subject of Steeley's barb, "The Doomsayer" Kyle Lee. Dressed in blue jeans and an Alan Trammell Detroit Tigers baseball jersey, the man with two matches makes his way down to the ring. He takes his time getting there as the Detroit fans welcome him back to the Silverdome. He walks the long way around along the dasherboards and accepts the house microphone from Henshall. Before going any further, he says one thing.] KL: One year. [Lee slides under the bottom rope and takes his proper position to speak.] KL: In the single span of a year everything can change. Let's take baseball for example. [A smirk as the Detroit faithful know where this is going.] KL: Last year your Tigers flirted with history in regards to being the worst team ever to take their positions on a baseball diamond. A year later they're in sight of a pennant. SS: Finally! He's making sense! KL: And back home, you've got the Rangers. When they unloaded A-Rod, it was a forgone conclusion that they'd easily be looking at 100 loses. [Another smirk.] KL: It's a year later and they're in first place. [A nice, respectful "[MEEP] the Yankees" pop. Really, deep down, everybody hates the Yankees.] KL: It was a year ago that I was wanting the opportunity to fight, the opportunity to prove I could be in the main event. All I needed was a chance to show that I was more than just a pencil pushing bastard sitting behind a desk. [The smile just won't go away.] KL: One single year. Show after show, Fatality has sought to beat me down, break my will and end my career. What started with Michael Augustine, led to Shane Matthews. What continued with Eugene Robinson led to the North American Championship. From there, it all came to this moment. One year later. The main event and a chance to win the World Championship. [Huge crowd pop for Lee.] KL: One year is a lot of time to think you know. Every day its something new. A different challenge is always around the corner and the variety of topics that run through your mind is just boggling. A year ago, I saw myself in constant fights with everybody and everything that could possibly be thrown at me. A year later I'm thinking all that plus one more thing. [A beat.] KL: Retirement. [A "what?" pop from the crowd.] KL: Southwest of Dallas is a place called Dublin. It's home to the oldest Dr. Pepper bottling plant in the country where they still make Dr. Pepper with real sugar. It's a groovy little town. I see myself settling down there, starting a farm and raising a family. I'll have bastard children running around being peppers and terrorizing my wife like little bastard children on a sugar high tend to do. And every June, when they change the name of the town to Dr. Pepper, TX to celebrate the anniversary of the opening of the plant, they'll have a parade. And every June when they have this parade, in it will be the man named "The Doomsayer" Kyle Lee. And when little Billy Joe McDublin sips his Dr. Pepper and asks his father who that man is, that father will smile proudly as Dublin's most famous resident passes by. [A pause to change the tone of his voice, as if older.] KL: "Well son, that there is the man who at Gold Rush 2004 beat Derek Martin, Michael Augustine and Edmond Winston IV all in one night. That there is the man who won the UWF World Title and put Jessica Marshall and her so called Law right on it's ass." [The crowd goes absolutely nuts for the thought of the very prospect of what Lee promises in this story.] KL: And as little Billy gasps in horror because his father said "ass", that father will put an arm around his son's shoulder while sipping a Dr. Pepper of his very own. "Billy, let that man be a lesson. When you face the impossible, expect the impossible to happen." [Another huge pop for Lee.] KL: Well Billy, Johnny, Suzie... I say to you "Dead End" Derek Marin isn't the end he pretends to be. He's a thug who crossed a line he shouldn't have and in the grand scheme of things, he's just a warm up to the beginning of the second year. And to you Molly, Jaime, Henry... I say to you that I see Edmond Winston IV. And I tell you that I beat him. I say to you that I see Michael Augustine. I beat him too. [Lee holds up two fingers to the camera.] KL: Twice. All this because Jessica Marshall saw fit to put me against the impossible. [A long pause as the crowd builds into another frenzy.] KL: Well ladies and gentlemen, boys and girls... Expect the impossible to happen tonight. ["Bleed for Me" begins to play again as the crowd lets loose with its support. Lee tosses the microphone back to Henshall and makes his way back up the ramp to the back. Cut back to the announcers again.] SS: Does that guy ever shut up? GOLD RUSH '04 MAIN EVENT ____ ___ ___ _ _ FOR THE HEAVYWEIGHT CHAMPIONSHIP |___ \ / _ \ / _ \ | || | OF THE WORLD: __) || | | || | | || || |_ Michael Augustine[c] versus Kyle Lee / __/ | |_| || |_| ||__ _| versus Edmond Winston IV |_____| \___/ \___/ |_| WRITER: [name withheld] .......................................................................... DH: The following contest is scheduled for one fall, and is YOUR GOLD RUSH 2004 MAIN EVENT for the Heavyweight Championship of the World! [The level of excitement continues to rise and the crowd gets to its feet when the opening sounds of "Bleed For Me" by the Black Label Society comes over the PA system.] DH: Introducing first, from Dallas, Texas... Weighing in at two hundred and forty pounds... "DOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOMSAYEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEER" KYYYYYYYYLLLLLLE LLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEE! [The introductory screams of the song leads to Lee stepping back out from the locker rooms, dressed in the same black tights with dark blue trim he was in earlier. The audience begins to chant his name over and over again, as the Texan walks slowly down to the ring.] SS: Well it's a nice story, but if anybody thinks that man has a shot in hell of winning this match they're out of their friggin' minds. Lee already wrestled a match tonight and barely snuck past Derek Martin. He doesn't have a prayer in hell of beating both Michael Augustine and Edmond Winston tonight. DR: Perhaps he doesn't have a good shot to win, but the year long roll Kyle Lee has been on has made a believer out of quite a few people. SS: You're awfully quiet, Amy. AM: I'm still not completely believing that Kyle Lee is in the Gold Rush main event. What a long strange trip this past year's been. DR: Amen. [As Kyle gets about a quarter of the way down the ramp, he suddenly turns back towards the entrance and points. And all of a sudden, the audience EXPLODES into thunderous applause! As "Mockingbird" Nina Grimsson emerges from the back and joins Kyle on the ramp to walk the rest of the way to ringside.] SS: Hey! What the hell is this, Edmond banned Lee from having a manager! AM: No, he banned Tesla St. James from being in his corner for his matches. He didn't say anything about a substitute, and frankly I can't think of a single better choice for this match. [Nina and Lee walk down the ramp to the ringside area, and the Doomsayer looks up at the ring and takes a deep breath before sliding between the ropes. Again the chants of his name are shouted by the crowd and Kyle walks to the far ropes and leans against them with his eyes closed, deep in thought. Nina sits on the apron, as a quick shot of Tesla in the crowd, deep in Gulag Fraternity with her arms folded and a satisfied smirk on her face. And Savantage's "Sarejevo" blares over the speakers next, the anthem prompting the crowd to unleash a huge wave of booing as the familiar black limousine pulls through from the side of the stage area and rolls to a stop next to the ramp in the aisleway.] DH: And the other challenger, from Winston-Salem, North Carolina, he is a former heavyweight champion of the world, accompanied to the ring by Jessica "Fatality" Marshall... EEEEEDMOOOOOOOOOOOND WIIIIIIIIIIIIINSTOOOOOOOOOOOON THEEEEEEEE FOOOOOOOOUUUUUUUUURTTTTTTTTHHHHHHHH! [The limo driver exits the car first, walks to the back and holds the door open on the rear driver's side. Stepping out of the car first is Fatality, dressed to kill in a pair of blue and white leather pants and a black top. Seconds after her is EW4 himself, dressed in his usual ring attire. Big Tobacco and Fatality walk the rest of the way down the aisle to the ring and stand outside as Lee glares at the two of them with hatred.] AM: So all of a sudden my sister is accompanying Edmond to the ring? SS: Why not? Grimsson doesn't belong here at ringside either, this just makes things a little more even. AM: No, even would have seen Winston and Augustine having other matches before this tonight. Kyle's still got a hell of a mountain to climb if he's going to pull it off. DR: Even moreso when you consider the limo there in the aisle, no doubt loaded once with weapons no doubt. [As the challengers stare one another down, the loud ringing of bells kicks off AC/DC's "Hell's Bells" and prompts both of them to swing their attention to the entrance portal. The Children of the Evolution begin to pop like crazy as everyone else unleashes an ungodly round of boos.] DH: And the champion! From Northwest Philadelphia, Pennsylvania, he is accompanied to the ring by Allison Ivey and weighs in at two hundred and forty-seven pounds, he is the current, two time, heavyweight champion of the world... "AAAAAAAGOOOONYYYYYYYYYY" MICHAAAAAAAAAEEEEELLLLLL AAAAUGUUUUUUUUUSSSSSSSSTIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIIINNNNNNNNNNNNNNNE! [And without a lot of fanfare, the duo of Allison Ivey and Michael Augustine come walking out from the back, each dressed in black attire. Allison shows some of the wear from her match early in the evening, but she tries not to let it bother her as she walks in front of the champion. Augy wears a black Philadelphia Phillies jersey over his ring gear, and drags the championship belt at his side. Tossing it over the ropes as he comes to the edge of the apron, Augustine rolls into the ring and rises to give the Youngblood salute. The CoE returns the favor, while Winston simply gives the champion a different salute. The fan faction has also managed to unfurl a banner and place it over the ringside barrier, the message reading "Youngblood Law Applies Here".] DR: All three of these men have a volatile temper, when you put them together this may just become world war three. SS: MAY? [An uneasy calm comes over the ring as all three wrestlers stare at one another, nobody looking to make the first move. Lee is clearly in the worst shape at this point, still feeling the effects of earlier tonight. The referee calls for the bell and the match officially starts, but aside from Winston raising an eyebrow there's no movement from the trio.] AM: Well, I wasn't expecting a standoff to start, considering how thin Augustine's grip on sanity is and just how big of a jerk Winston can be. SS: You know, as I'm looking out in this crowd now I can't help but notice something. AM: What's that? SS: [pointing up] THAT. AM: Oh god... is that a blimp? [A quick cut shows that yes, in fact, it is a blimp high above the crowd. On the side of it is written "New Era in Professional Wrestling presents Gold Rush 2004".] AM: That's a little... unsettling. SS: Somebody's a fan of irony. DR: Wait, here we go! Augustine exploding on Kyle Lee with lefts and rights, and the Doomsayer is doing his best to keep up but after wrestling already tonight he's being worn down... SS: And all the while, Edmond gets to stand back and watch. He really is brilli- ACK! [Sam is stopped as Augustine turns around and nails Winston with a clothesline, sending him over the ropes to the outside! HEEL POP! An eneraged EW4 pops back to his feet, slamming his hands on the mat and trying to climb right back in but Fatality grabs him by the arm, trying to calm him down first. In the ring Augustine keeps the attack on up Lee, throwing him to the corner and unleashing more shots to the head and face. He goes for a stomp to the midsection but Kyle blocks it, spins Augustine around and comes back out of the corner himself with a clothesline to the back of the head. A suplex attempt is blocked, and now Winston chooses to return to the ring with a low blow, stunning Lee and allowing the ex-champion to snap his neck off the top rope. Lee rebounds right to the canvas, gasping for air and Winston ducks between the ropes, rejoining the match and throws Augustine into the ropes. He swings at him with a clothesline, Augy ducks it and comes back with a boot to the gut, tries a DDT and Edmond catches the champion with a northern lights suplex into a cradle! 1! 2! Frantic kickout from the Youngblood, who sits up only to be caught by the legs and forced into a half Boston position on the mat. Edmond doesn't hang on too long thanks to a running kneelift by the Doomsayer, as the crowd explodes and Kyle gets EW4 in a lateral press.] DR: One! Kickout, and the pin broken up by Augustine at the same time. The nature of a three-way dance like this forces all three participants to stay close to the action. AM: Especially the champion. And if Augustine wasn't paranoid before... [Augy drags Lee to his feet first, battering him with an uppercut and tosses him to the ropes. Off the rebound Augustine ducks his head and Lee is forced to leapfrog over, right into the waiting arms of Winston who sends Kyle crashing and burning to the floor! Heel pop! As the Mockingbird helps him back up, Augustine and Winston exchange hard punches back and forth and it's Big Tobacco who takes control after a kick to the groin, and then directs Augustine into the nearest cornerpost shoulder-first. A rare scream from the Youngblood as his shoulder meets the steel, and Edmond catches him from behind with a schoolboy rollup. 1! 2! KICKOUT!] DR: Huge escape from Michael Augustine, but his shoulder hit the post quite hard and I don't think he's going to be able to bounce back from it immediately. SS: What's even worse for him is that's the same side as his injured hand! Edmond's making damn sure one side of his body is weaker than the other. [Fatality begins to yell at the referee, and because of it Allison Ivey begins to yell at Fatality. The two women keep hurling insults loudly at one another, while Lee rolls back into the ring and surprises Augustine with an elbow to the face in the corner. Edmond tries to sneak up on Kyle with a running splash but Lee sidesteps, and the Billion Dollar Bastard smashes into the Youngblood instead. Staggering backwards, Edmond is caught and rolled up... 1! 2! Shoulder up! A round of boos comes up and Lee is the first to rise, but he's caught by a wicked lariat out of the corner from Augustine. Another scream from Augustine as he grasps his shoulder, the pain coursing all the way through it now from continued abuse.] AM: Doesn't bode well for Michael or the title, if he's in that much pain from a clothesline. SS: Yup, Edmond [meep]ed him up good. DR: Winston returns to his feet now and scoops Augustine up on his shoulders, dumps him out to the floor with a fireman's carry! SS: Sure, even with the bad arm Edmond knows he has an easier chance of winning if he beats the hell out of Lee instead. I told you he was brilliant. [Edmond waits for Kyle rise, groggy from the lariat and a pair of knife-edge chops softens him up for EW4 and a front facelock. Lee blocks a suplex takedown, but a shot to the ribs doesn't prevent Winston from driving him into the mat with a single-arm DDT and then turning it into a three-hold armbar!] DR: No More Mister Nice Guy by Edmond! SS: Lee's gonna tap! The title is back where it belongs! AM: He hasn't tapped out yet, Kyle would sooner pass out than give him the satisfaction. SS: Give him about thirty seconds then. [Lee tries to reach the ropes with his free hand, but EW4 has it locked in tight. Nina Grimsson leans down and gets in Lee's face, shouting at him not to give in, not to surrender. Lee continues to fight, but he begins to fade as Edmond's face gets brighter and brighter...] DR: AUGUSTINE DROPS AN AXEHANDLE! He saved Kyle Lee but in the process he saved his championship as well! AM: His only goal is to win this match, and leave with that belt. You can bet he doesn't give a damn about Kyle or his well-being. [Augustine grabs Edmond, gritting his teeth and yanking him up with his damaged arm to lift him up and deliver a kneebreaker! It's Edmond who now screams in pain, collapsing and holding his leg as Augustine drops an elbow into the inside of his thigh. Kyle rolls to the ropes and begins to lift himself up, but as he does he finds something, or someone, has grabbed hold of his leg.] AM: Dammit, what's Jess doing?!? Let go of him! DR: It didn't take too long for Jessica Marshall to get involved in this match... but here comes Nina Grimsson! [The whole Silverdome explodes as the Mockingbird rushes around the ring just as Fatality lets go of Lee and tries to escape into the safety of the crowd... ....but finds her path blocked by Tesla St. James and Lisa Drake!] SS: Run Jess, run! Not that way! DR: Extreme Measures is chasing the co-owner of the UWF up the aisle and this crowd is exploding! [Jessica, who looks like a deer caught in the headlights, stops abruptly and changes directions, heading up the ramp towards the locker rooms at full speed. Even in the ring, Augustine and Winston watch as Drake and Tesla chase after her, the crowd chanting: "KICK HER ASS! KICK HER ASS! KICK HER ASS! KICK HER ASS!" "KICK HER ASS! KICK HER ASS! KICK HER ASS! KICK HER ASS!" "KICK HER ASS! KICK HER ASS! KICK HER ASS! KICK HER ASS!" "KICK HER ASS! KICK HER ASS! KICK HER ASS! KICK HER ASS!" Winston glares towards the back, and completely misses Augustine rushing straight ahead at him to send Big Tobacco through the ropes to the arena floor! Augustine grabs a still groggy Kyle and scoops him up for a bodyslam, kicks him in the ribs a few times and pulls him up by the head into a facelock.] DR: The champion tightens his grip, and hooks Lee for a suplex... no, cradle brainbuster! He drove Lee straight down into the mat with it, and covers! ONE! TWO! [But a foot on the ropes breaks the count! HUGE POP! Kyle rolls onto his stomach, and is subsequently dragged right of the ring by Winston, then floored with a hard shot to the skull.] AM: This is just not a level playing field. Kyle wasn't anywhere near ready for this match and it's just been Winston and Augustine trading off in beating him down! SS: He knew the rules coming in, and he still showed up. Don't you feel a bit sorry for Lee. [Augustine exits the ring near his manager, Allison, and slowly creeps around the side of the ring to where Winston and Lee are. A concerned Nina Grimsson returns to her position in Kyle's corner after standing halfway up the ramp, but a small grin creeps onto her face when Augustine catches Winston with a clothesline after Allison baits him into charging around the corner. Now again Augustine is clutching his arm and gritting his teeth in pain, while Edmond grabs the apron and tries to pick himself up. Around the corner is Lee, on his hands and knees and still trying to keep himself afloat in this match.] DR: All three men out on the arena floor, and with who they are it's even more dangerous than normal. Kyle Lee opening up on Augustine with a series of punches... poke to the eye from the champion... [CRACK! The crowd explodes as Augustine attempts to clothesline Kyle, but the Doomsayer sidesteps and the Youngblood's forearm and shoulder meet the steel of the ringpost once again! CRASH!] AM: Winston threw him into the ringsteps! Champ down, champ down! DR: Winston and Lee trading blows out here very near to us, and Kyle lands a kneelift! [Lee grabs EW4 by the head and drives it into the announcer's table hard! SMASH! SMASH! SMASH! SMASH! A dazed Winston staggers away, rolling back into the ring as the Doomsayer smiles through the fatigue, raising an arm to the crowd.] AM: It looks like Kyle is getting a second wind here. SS: On the mileage he's put on tonight, that's more like a sixth or seventh wind. [Kyle climbs onto the ring apron and meets Winston with another shot to the head, staggering Big Tobacco. Lee slingshots himself over the ropes... and gets caught in a tilt-a-whirl slam! Edmond hooks a leg quickly... 1! 2! Kickout!] DR: Lee dodges defeat again! Win or lose, he's leaving nothing to chance here. Lee and Winston both back to their feet, Kyle ducks a shot and a kick to the thigh! Edmond stumbles back, legsweep takedown and here's a Half Boston... no, it's a camel clutch and crab combo! AM: That's the Preordained Downfall! One of Kyle's old signature moves from when he was the Prophet! SS: Oh you mean from the time when he was still getting sympathy sex from you in the wheelchair? [SLAP!] DR: That answers that... Augustine breaks it up with a knee into Kyle Lee's kidneys! SS: He'll be eating through straws tonight. [Augustine lifts Kyle Lee up and hooks him with an abdominal stretch, but his grip isn't very good on account of his damaged shoulder and arm as Lee is able to hiptoss his way free. EW4 struggles to his feet and grabs Lee from behind with a waistlock, but Kyle grabs the ropes and keeps himself from being suplexed over. Edmond tries again to hit it, but Lee hangs on. Augustine pulls himself up and nails Kyle in the stomach with a kick to break his grip, but then belts Winston as well. He dives over the both of them...] DR: Augustine with a sunset flip... AND WINSTON EXECUTES A GERMAN SUPLEX ON KYLE LEE! Both men have their shoulders down! ONE! TWO! Double Kickout! AM: That was a great move by Augustine, no doubt about it. He nearly pinned both of his opponents at once! [Slowly, all three of them begin to get to their feet and it becomes a three way brawl with punches being thrown in several different directions. Edmond tries to bounce off the ropes but gets tripped by Allison Ivey, who like the other Youngbloods before her flips off Winston after drawing his ire. He leaves himself open to Augustine and a running knee between the shoulderblades which sends EW4 crashing to the outside apron. Kyle in the meantime catches Augustine with a schoolboy rollup... 1! 2! Kickout! HEEL POP! Augustine gets up, enraged, and swings away at Lee who ducks and shoves Augustine straight into Edmond who's still on the apron. He goes flying and smashes sternum-first into the boards, while Lee hits a swinging neckbreaker. Again he covers... 1! 2! Foot on the ropes! The Youngblood starts to get up but he's snared into an Oklahoma Roll... 1! 2! KICKOUT! "LEE! LEE! LEE! LEE!" "LEE! LEE! LEE! LEE!" "LEE! LEE! LEE! LEE!" "LEE! LEE! LEE! LEE!"] AM: Can you believe this?!? He's already been in a streetfight this evening, and yet Kyle is taking the champion to school repeatedly with these close pinfalls! SS: Yeah, just wait until he hits the wall. And I mean that literally... Augustine's about to run him headfirst into a brick wall out in the parking lot. [The weary champion struggles to one knee, his shoulder throbbing... ....and removes his gloves, elbowpads, and kneepads.] DR: Now what is this all about? SS: Maybe it's weighing him down. AM: Think again. Every time he's got a big match, Michael Augustine loves to have that little advantage by loading up his gear. This has to be his way of proving to the world that he can take care of Kyle straight up, one on one. SS: I still prefer my wall idea. [Lee and Augustine stand face to face in the ring, and then start to unleash a volley of shots at one another. Several of them land and Augustine grabs Kyle's face, driving it downwards as his knee comes upwards. Face meets bone and Lee collapses in the champion's hands, as Augustine sets up and delivers a spine-rattling powerbomb straight to the canvas. 1! 2! 3- But just before the referee's hand comes down it's Lee who surprises Augustine one more time...] AM: BASTARD GRINDER! HE'S LOCKED IN THE BASTARD GRINDER ON AUGUSTINE'S SHOULDER! DR: Another of Kyle Lee's signature submission holds... there is nowhere for the champion to go now! SS: Help him Allison! [Augustine's eyes grow wide as the ripping and tearing of his shoulder pulsates all through his body, and Lee reaches back as far as he can to twist and pull with the Bastard Grinder. Augustine's hand hovers just above the canvas surface... .... .... .... ______________CRAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAACK!______________ And the camera pans up, as EW4 stands above both of his exhausted opponents.] DR: CHAIRSHOT TO THE BACK OF KYLE LEE'S SKULL! DAMMIT! SS: That's not just a chairshot! That's a friggin' _GOLDEN_ chairshot! AM: I thought I recognized the damn thing, it's the chair he used at Gold Rush last year to win the title! Don't tell me history is about to repeat itself! [Edmond raises the solid gold chair over his head again and swiftly brings it down across the outstretched arm of Michael Augustine... _CRRRRRRRRRRRRRRRACK!_] AM: Oh my god! I think he broke his arm in half! Did you hear that sound?!? SS: Even if Mikey keeps the belt tonight, I guess Allison's not gonna be getting any of the good lovi- DR: Arabian Facebuster across Kyle Lee's face! Edmond Winston with the cover! ONE! TWO! AM: Not like this! DR: THRE- NO! Augustine with a last second effort to save the title! [Edmond stands up, pure hatred written across his face as he grabs Augustine by the head and the badly injured shoulder. The champion groans in pain as he absorbs a knife-edge chop across the chest and then his arm is wrapped up in the ropes as Edmond backs him into the corner. EW4 with another series of chops, but Augustine lashes out with a headbutt to the bridge of the nose, one that sounds more like the crack of a shotgun blast! Edmond staggers but manages to grasp the ropes... And with all of his remaining strength drives his knee square into the groin of the Youngblood, the force driving Augustine two feet into the air.] DR: ... AM: Talk about impact! SS: ... AM: Guys? [Edmond heaves and places Augustine up onto the top turnbuckle, and begins to climb after him. He stands on the second rope for a few seconds, and then snags his leg with Augustine's while hooking Augy's head under his arm and then falls backwards...] DR: POWER TRIP! POWER TRIP BY EDMOND WINSTON! ONE! TWO! AUGUSTINE WITH A HAND ON THE ROPES! [An enormous heel pop from the Children of Evolution rises from the crowd as a visibly frustrated EW4 gets to his feet again and begins to drive knee after knee after knee into the face of Augustine. And all of a sudden, the building is plunged into darkness! Strobe lights randomly flash through the darkness and surging hazy purple smoke as LL Cool J's "Mama Said Knock You Out" hits the speakers to blast through the speakers at full tilt. Needless to say, all the action in the ring has been ground to a halt.] SS: What the hell?!? AM: We know the music well, it belongs to Alex Extreme! But he was thrown out of the building a little while ago! [Through the smoky purple haze and pyro walks Mr. Excitement himself, wearing a black and neon blue leather jacket, a black t-shirt with a big huge clumsily splashed big X in neon purple on it, Ray Ban wafer shades and a pair of neon blue and black swirled spandex pants. The spotlights swirl all around him as the lights begin to come up, enough to show Augustine on his hands and knees and EW4 standing up, facing the entrance. Extreme stays up at the stage area, but has something in his hand. He points upwards, up towards the New Era Blimp.] DR: Alex Extreme has issues with both Augustine _and_ Winston, but why is he... SS: LOOK AT THE BLIMP! Extreme has a damn remote control, he's gonna ruin this match with the friggin' blimp! [Indeed, the blimp begins to move through the air, closer and closer to the ring as a frantic Winston tries to point this out to the referee. The official for his part does see the blimp, and points up at Extreme. Alex waves back, and the official leaves the ring to go after him up on the stage. Through the blinding lights Winston smirks and raises two middle fingers up at Extreme, shouting "Your crap won't work tonight, Extreme!"] SS: Leave it to Alex Extreme to try some kind of funny crap like using a blimp in this match, thank god Edmond is way too smart to fall for it though. Kick him out, ref, kick him- AM: LOOK BEHIND WINSTON! IT'S ALEX EXTREME! [The crowd _ROARS_ as the lights come up full, and standing behind EW4 on the turnbuckles is the one and only Extreme, dressed in street clothes and wielding a familiar object to the tobacco scion. Edmond turns around around...] SS: DAMMIT, EXTREME HAS HIS TITANIUM GOLF CLUB! [...and blasts him in the top of the head with the club! Winston's eyes roll back in his head and he collapses in a heap to the mat as Alex stands over him.] DR: The real Alex Extreme has struck down Edmond Winston the Fourth with his own golf club... but who the hell is that up there? AM: Oh my god, that's Job Holly! [The official grabs the imposter by the arm and indeed, it is the face of Job Holly who he sees. Taken aback, the official is at a loss for words while in the ring the world champion has gotten back to his feet. He sees Extreme and his eyes blaze with fury, then Augustine charges straight for Alex!] DR: Alex ducks- SHOT TO THE MIDSECTION! OH MY GOD, EXTREME TAKES DOWN MICHAEL AUGUSTINE WITH THE GOLF CLUB AS WELL! AM: Payback on the Youngbloods, bigtime! [Alex tosses the club, and heads for the turnbuckles. Jumping off he lands on top of Augy with a moonsault legdrop, leaving him down and out before rolling from the ring. The official is on his way back down after having thrown Job Holly out of the arena... ....just as Kyle Lee begins to crawl across the canvas, having come to at the right moment and throws an arm across Augustine.] AM: Oh my god, is Kyle going to get the win?!? DR: COVER! ONE! TWO! THREE! NO! THE OFFICIAL SAYS TWO AND A HALF! MY GOD WHAT A MATCH! [The dissapointment courses through the crowd as Lee sits up, soaked with his own sweat and the fatigue really setting in. Winston very slowly is coming back to his feet at about the same time as Kyle, and Lee grabs his legs and trips Winston up... ....before catapulting him over the top rope... ....and right on top of the gold chair!] AM: Alright, a little poetic justice! I- no, watch out Kyle! DR: AUGUSTINE TO HIS FEET- SS: And severely pissed off! DR: A KICK TO THE STOMACH, SOUL BREAKER... AND HE CAN'T HANG ON! OH MY GOD! AM: GONE TO TEXAS! DR: GONE TO TEXAS OUT OF NOWHERE! ONE! TWO! THREE! [The Pontiac Silverdome erupts as the official calls for the bell, and one tired bastard rolls away as Augustine kicks out just a few microseconds too late.] DR: We have a new World Champion! AM: HE'S DONE IT IT! SS: I DON'T BELIEVE THIS! [The referee takes the glittering championship belt from the attendent and raises the Doomsayer's arm.] DH: Here is your winner... AND NNNNNNNNNNNNNNNNEEEEEEEEEEEEEEW HEAVYWEIGHT CHAMPION OF THE WORLD... "DOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOOMSAAAAAAAAAYEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEER" KYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYYLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLE LLLLLLLLLLLLLLLLEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEEE! AM: LEE DID IT! LEE DID IT! KYLE LEE HAS DONE THE IMPOSSIBLE! [The crowd erupts again as Kyle Lee's hand is raised in victory. The Texan looks utterly exhausted, but the weight of the gold in his hands seems to electrify him. He raises the World Championship high amidst a score of flashbulbs. HUGE POP!] DR: Who would have thought? Congratulations, Kyle Lee! You have more than earned that World Championship, and the respect of every single person in the arena tonight. [Augustine rolls out of the ring and begins to slam his hand on the apron repeatedly, in a state more pissed off than we've seen in quite some time. His hand begins to bleed, by now obviously numb to Augustine. Allison tries her best to calm him down, but Augustine is in a white rage and she's not even registering with him as he storms up the side of the ramp.] SS: I would hate to be in that Youngblood dressing room when Michael gets back there... run Drakes run! [Now Tesla St. James comes running down the ramp as "Bleed For Me" plays over the PA. Her face is one of mixed emotions; concern for the fresh Hell her charge has gone through, and elation that he has won the biggest prize in the sport today. She slips into the ring and stands before Kyle Lee... ....who scoops the leggy redhead up in a tight embrace! HUGE "COUPLE OF THE YEAR" POP!] SS: WOOHOO! He's gonna be getting some victory se- [SLAP!] AM: Show the new champion the respect he's due, Sam. Fighting Derek Martin was a hard enough battle, but this was a war. [A war that is not yet over... in fact, a war that has only just begun. Cut to Edmond Winston IV who is on his knees outside the ring, cradling his head and moaning in pain. He wipes fresh blood from his eyes, and looks into the ring with a truly jealous glare.] DR: Better luck next time, Mister Winston! AM: It takes a little more than owning the Federation to be its Champion. I think that's a lesson that is sinking in to that evil [MEEP!]'s head right now. [The cameras close in on Lee and Tes as the pair takes the time to pose for a few photos. Lee is still catching his breath, but manages to have a genuine smile for the cameras. He and Tesla joke quietly as they hold the World Championship aloft, and the crowd is eating every minute up!] DR: I don't care if it costs me my job to say this, but I'd like to go on record as saying that it's about time that the Universal Wrestling Federation had a Champion who was worth cheering for. It's been far too long. AM: Hear hear! SS: You wanna know what I think? DR & AM: NO! SS: Jeez, you don't have to be so mean about it. [The music starts again from the beginning as Lee and Tesla St. James are joined in the ring by the Sons of Cacaphony and Extreme Measures, all looking to celebrate the victory of the new Man. Outside the ring a still dazed EW4 limps back up the side of the aisle to his limo, and climbs up on the hood of it. He locks eyes with Lee, who with the remaining strength his body possesses, raises the belt over his head one more time.] DR: Fans, we are out of time! For everybody here at the Silverdome, for the brand new heavyweight champion and for the UWF, I'm David Rogers, goodnight! [Cut to the ring as Tesla begins to strap the championship belt around Lee's waist, with a volley of fireworks going off overhead. Fade to sweet championship black.]
